For fiscal year 2027, the DOE's total proposed budget is 5.58 billion, reflecting an increase of 1.14 billion or 25.7%.

**[00:31:55.940] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000215)  
Compared with fiscal year 2026 GA of 4.43 billion.

**[00:32:01.220] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000216)  
From 2022 to 2026, new appropriations generally maintained the largest share in the DOE's total budget, from 59.4 to 68.9%, versus automatic appropriations of about 31.1 to 40.6%.

**[00:32:20.220] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000217)  
But for 2027, new appropriations decreased by ₱935 million.

**[00:32:25.940] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000218)  
And account for only 36.4% of the total recommended budget, while 63.6% comes from the SAGF.

**[00:32:37.580] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000219)  
In other words, the department's budgetary requirements will be heavily supported through the SAGF, sourced from the revenues from energy service contracts and other collections of the department.

**[00:32:51.740] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000220)  
This chart shows the DOE…

**[00:32:54.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000221)  
Before you leave again, I just want to comment.

**[00:32:55.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000222)  
So, for the first time since 2022, which is the last six years, the new appropriations will actually be less.

**[00:33:17.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000223)  
This is very important because basically, DOE is more self-sustaining than you were in the past.

**[00:33:25.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000224)  
And I think compared to many other agencies, malaking bagay yun na.

**[00:33:28.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000225)  
So, it's really something that I wanted to emphasize.

**[00:33:31.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000226)  
And as you said, the reason is, meron kayong revenues na pumapasok to support this.

**[00:33:38.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000227)  
So, I want to emphasize this because it's a good thing.

**[00:33:41.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000228)

**[00:33:42.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000229)

**[00:34:21.240] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000243)  
This chart shows the DOE's budget utilization rate.

**[00:34:25.240] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000244)  
From 2022 to 31 August 2026.

**[00:34:28.760] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000245)  
Upon completion of procurement of the mobile energy system and the DOE automation project, which are already undergoing procurement, the obligation rate will increase to 67%.

**[00:34:40.680] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000246)  
The DOE expects to reach at least 95.89% utilization rate for 2026.

**[00:34:48.600] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000247)  
The increase in the DOE's recommended 2027 budget from the current year's budget level.

**[00:34:55.240] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000248)  
It's in large part owing to increased funding for these three major projects.

**[00:34:59.820] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000249)  
The Philippine Gradiometry and Seismic Survey Project, or simply the Gradiometry Project, the Mobile Energy Systems, or MES, under the Total Electrification Project, and the ISSP, which includes the DOE Automation Project.

**[00:35:18.920] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000250)  
The Gradiometry Project is the department’s initiative to improve resource mapping and prospect

**[00:35:25.240] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000251)  
identification,

**[00:35:25.820] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000252)  
Reduce exploration risk and help attract greater upstream energy investments in the Philippines by generating higher quality geophysical data across priority petroleum basins.

**[00:35:38.940] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000253)  
The Grid Geometry Project covers 120,000 square kilometers.

**[00:35:43.620] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000254)  
The area could support up to 120 individual exploration service contracts.

**[00:35:48.900] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000255)  
Using data from the recently awarded exploration contracts,

**[00:35:53.360] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000256)  
just 10% of

**[00:35:55.220] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000257)  
the potential 120 contracts alone would already account for approximately ₱16.8 billion of potential private-sector investments.

**[00:36:04.740] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000258)  
And that is not even counting potential oil and gas discoveries.

**[00:36:11.120] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000259)  
Under the Total Electrification Project, the department is procuring MES that will provide...

**[00:36:17.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000260)  
One last before you go to that again, let's go back to this because I was very interested in this when it was presented.

**[00:36:23.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000261)  
The Philippine Gradiometry and Seismic Survey Project investment is ₱2.7 billion.

**[00:36:31.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000262)  
And that's basically to be surveying that area that was blocked off.

**[00:36:35.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000263)  
And I think, Secretary, you explained that even the selection of the area,

**[00:36:41.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000264)  
because it's costly to survey the whole area, is very strategic.

**[00:36:46.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000265)  
Because these are the areas that there's a big chance of making a discovery, correct?

**[00:36:51.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000266)  
Yes.

**[00:36:53.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000267)  
So you want to be sure that this item is secured in the budget, right?

**[00:36:57.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000268)  
This is not an additional budget you're asking for. It's already in the NEP.

**[00:37:02.120] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000269)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[00:37:02.820] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000270)  
Okay.

**[00:37:03.540] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000271)  
And just briefly, Sandy, our in-house expert on this, can explain why.

**[00:37:10.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000272)  
Yes, explain it very quickly on the record because our briefing is not live.

**[00:37:16.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000273)  
I want people to know that we're investing in this kind of exploration.

**[00:37:22.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000274)  
Yes.

**[00:37:23.220] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000275)  
So, first point really is [unclear] po ng department kung saan natin dadalhin itong bagong acquisition ng data.

**[00:37:31.960] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000276)  
So, for the information, the last major exercise like this was in the 80s

**[00:37:37.640] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000277)  
na nag-acquire ng data ang Philippine government over our basins.

**[00:37:42.600] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000278)  
And it was funded through a World Bank loan.

**[00:37:45.800] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000279)  
So, major milestone that after so many decades, we are again investing.

**[00:37:53.220] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000280)  
In our own basins.

**[00:37:55.580] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000281)  
The main criteria why we picked these basins is parang nakalimutan sila because the decision in the 70s and 80s was that these areas were gas-prone.

**[00:38:08.280] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000282)  
At that time, natural gas po ay mahirap gawing commercial.

**[00:38:13.580] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000283)  
Walang masyadong bumibili.

**[00:38:15.000] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000284)  
When gas became a very valuable commodity beginning in the 2000s and up to the present time.

**[00:38:22.880] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000285)  
And in fact, kailangan ng Pilipinas, [unclear] namin itong mga areas na ito, nakalimutan dahil gas-prone daw.

**[00:38:30.880] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000286)  
But it could really matter to the future of the country if we find more indigenous gas in this what we call forgotten areas.

**[00:38:39.780] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000287)  
Thank you po.

**[00:38:40.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000288)  
Thank you for that.

**[00:38:41.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000289)  
Kasi I remember when I sponsored our bill on NatGas, we put on record that sadly,

**[00:38:51.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000290)  
the height of exploration, investment was in the 70s.

**[00:38:58.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000291)  
And then, hindi na ho naulit yun.

**[00:38:59.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000292)  
So, you're tying in that information we have na 80s pala yung last data collection.

**[00:39:06.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000293)  
But it makes sense that at that time, since hindi pa NatGas yung uso, hindi muna tinuloy.

**[00:39:14.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000294)  
Tapos ngayon, kailangan na nating—NatGas is really a very valuable commodity now.

**[00:39:20.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000295)  
So, now's a good time.

**[00:39:21.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000296)  
For this to really be, for this to be the focus.

**[00:39:25.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000297)  
Okay.

**[00:39:26.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000298)  
But later on, you'll also mention the development also and what, exploration for geothermal, no?

**[00:39:33.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000299)  
Kasi malaki din yung investment natin dati.

**[00:39:37.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000300)  
So, later on, I think that's part of your presentation naman.

**[00:39:41.080] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000301)  
Madam Chair, if I may, one last point.

**[00:39:43.920] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000302)  
Actually, when we talk to investors, malaking push din yung nangyari sa NatGas law.

**[00:39:49.300] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000303)  
Because, ang nangyari doon,

**[00:39:51.140] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000304)  
it really bridged the gap between yung upstream, yung pag-explore,

**[00:39:55.160] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000305)  
and saan natin ibebenta.

**[00:39:57.720] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000306)  
What happened with our NatGas law was the market was assured

**[00:40:01.000] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000307)  
because we said in the law that preference ang indigenous gas.

**[00:40:05.940] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000308)  
So, very important factor when we go out and meet the investors.

**[00:40:09.980] DOE Undersecretary Alessandro O. Sales** (asr-000309)  
Thank you po.

**[00:40:10.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000310)  
And for those just following now, I remember the discussion was,

**[00:40:16.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000311)  
the emphasis here, the reality here is it's such a big investment because,

**[00:40:20.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000312)

**[00:40:25.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000313)  
Hindi tayo sure, di ba?

**[00:40:26.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000314)  
Hindi tayo sure.

**[00:40:27.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000315)  
So, they may spend a lot and not get anything.

**[00:40:30.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000316)  
So, to be able to tell them that if you do find gas,

**[00:40:33.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000317)  
priority in the Philippines to purchase their own indigenous gas versus yung imported.

**[00:40:39.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000318)  
Importante yun.

**[00:40:39.900] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000319)  
Kasi, for investors, yun ang hinahanap nila na ano yung return of investment.

**[00:40:44.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000320)

**[00:40:46.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000321)  
But, at least we're saying, pag may nakita,

**[00:40:48.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000322)  
then, I mean,

**[00:40:50.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000323)  
those who are investing are in the industry naman eh.

**[00:40:53.120] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000324)  
So, they know the risk.

**[00:40:54.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000325)

**[00:40:58.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000326)  
So, yeah.

**[00:40:59.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000327)  
So, I'm glad that you have this investment now for this project to proceed.

**[00:43:34.380] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-000406)

**[00:43:59.260] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000419)

**[00:44:00.460] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000420)  
Next slide. Finally, we present

**[00:44:02.420] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000421)  
a summary of the department's major plans

**[00:44:04.420] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000422)  
and programs. First,

**[00:44:06.600] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000423)  
we accelerate energy resource development.

**[00:44:09.420] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000424)  
The biggest investment

**[00:44:10.340] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000425)  
of the DOE is on the

**[00:44:12.100] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000426)  
grid geometry project, which is

**[00:44:14.360] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000427)  
expected to significantly increase oil

**[00:44:16.480] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000428)  
and gas explorations in the country.

**[00:44:19.000] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000429)  
We are implementing the

**[00:44:20.460] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000430)  
Philippine Geothermal Derisking Facility

**[00:44:22.700] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000431)  
to revive geothermal

**[00:44:24.480] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000432)  
exploration in the Philippines.

**[00:44:26.420] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000433)  
We are also conducting additional

**[00:44:28.300] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000434)  
green energy auctions

**[00:44:29.760] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000435)  
such as waste to energy,

**[00:44:32.480] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000436)  
onshore and offshore wind,

**[00:44:34.540] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000437)  
geothermal, agro-solar,

**[00:44:37.260] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000438)  
solar on stilts,

**[00:44:38.800] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000439)  
solar on irrigation canals,

**[00:44:40.540] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000440)  
and off-grid areas.

**[00:44:42.380] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000441)  
Nuclear energy remains part of the

**[00:44:44.500] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000442)  
DOE's long-term energy strategy,

**[00:44:47.380] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000443)  
functioning as cheaper

**[00:44:48.500] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000444)  
and more reliable base load

**[00:44:50.340] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000445)  
power. The mid-merit

**[00:44:52.260] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000446)  
natural gas capacity auction

**[00:44:53.980] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000447)  
is targeted to be completed

**[00:44:56.120] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000448)  
by December 2026.

**[00:44:58.500] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000449)  
We also have major plans

**[00:45:00.000] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000450)  
to ensure energy security, including the deployment of MES, establishment of a state-owned strategic fuel reserve,

**[00:45:10.160] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000451)  
formulation of the Visayas, Mindoro, and Palawan energy master plans,

**[00:45:15.840] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000452)  
implementation of policies for generation companies' accountability, review of systems laws-related policies and programs,

**[00:45:23.380] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000453)  
push for government-wide adoption of electric vehicles or EVs, and deployment of EV charging stations.

**[00:45:32.120] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000454)  
We also have several key projects to strengthen governance, including the full digitalization, automation, and AI transformation of DOE,

**[00:45:42.660] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000455)  
the establishment of the DOE Center for Energy Excellence, comprising of the Energy Museum, Energy Academy, and R&D Center,

**[00:45:53.380] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000456)  
the strengthening of DOE Manpower, the modernization of the DOE Nationwide Command Center,

**[00:46:00.480] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000457)  
and the upgrading of DOE facilities to achieve net-zero energy consumption.

**[00:46:06.560] DOE Undersecretary Giovanni Carlo J. Bacordo** (asr-000458)  
Madam Chair, Honorable Members of the Committee, this ends my presentation. Thank you for your kind attention.

**[00:48:21.240] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000495)  
We just discovered that many of our plants, coal-fired power plants, gas plants, and so on, are maintained by foreigners.

**[00:48:32.420] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000496)

**[00:48:40.980] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000497)

**[00:48:45.080] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000498)

**[00:48:51.240] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000499)

**[00:48:52.960] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000500)

**[00:48:57.220] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000501)

**[00:49:02.900] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000502)

**[00:49:06.520] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000503)

**[00:49:15.140] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000505)

**[00:49:18.780] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000506)

**[00:49:21.240] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000507)

**[00:51:04.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000545)

**[00:51:11.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000547)  
What's our current energy mix?

**[00:51:17.440] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000548)

**[00:51:19.720] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000549)

**[00:51:20.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000550)

**[00:51:22.040] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000551)

**[00:51:24.680] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000552)

**[00:51:30.680] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000553)

**[00:51:35.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000554)

**[00:51:40.380] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000555)

**[00:51:45.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000556)

**[00:51:50.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000557)  
So, can you...

**[00:51:50.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000558)

**[00:51:53.860] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000559)

**[00:51:57.940] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000560)

**[00:52:01.200] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000561)  
Madam Chair.

**[00:52:02.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000562)  
Let me just acknowledge Senator Erwin Tulfo.

**[00:52:05.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000563)

**[00:52:08.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000564)

**[00:52:10.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000565)

**[00:52:10.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000566)  
I don't have any opening statement, Madam Chair.

**[00:52:13.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000567)

**[00:52:14.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000568)

**[00:52:16.640] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000569)  
Madam Chair, from 2022 to 2024,

**[00:52:19.040] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000570)  
our renewable energy in the generation mix in megawatt hours was 22%.

**[00:52:24.720] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000571)  
Last year, 2025, it jumped to 25%.

**[00:52:28.940] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000572)  
It's a 3% increase.

**[00:52:31.300] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000573)  
So, we figured based on our plans and the green energy auction results,

**[00:52:37.300] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000574)  
by 2030, we will reach 35% of renewable energy in the generation mix.

**[00:52:44.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000575)  
And that's the target?

**[00:52:46.820] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000576)  
Yes, ma'am.

**[00:52:47.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000577)  
Okay.

**[00:52:47.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000578)  
And in terms of pricing,

**[00:52:49.700] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000580)  
banggitin mo na,

**[00:52:50.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000581)  
kasi on record.

**[00:52:52.460] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000582)  
Dati po yung coal, mas mura siya kesa sa solar.

**[00:52:55.520] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000583)

**[00:52:59.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000584)  
So, I'll repeat.

**[00:53:00.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000585)  
Solar is now cheaper than coal.

**[00:53:02.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000586)  
So, in our desire to push for more RE, more renewables, more sustainable,

**[00:53:09.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000587)  
we are on track and it should happen because hindi na madaling sabihin na

**[00:53:15.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000588)  
mas mura ang coal, kailangan natin umasa sa coal.

**[00:53:17.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000589)  
And can you also add to the discussion?

**[00:53:19.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000590)  
So, people understand, no?

**[00:53:21.700] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000591)  
Because it's very rare that you get a full picture.

**[00:53:25.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000592)  
One of the reasons why RE, renewable energy, was difficult to push was because of the storage.

**[00:53:32.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000593)  
So, kung walang araw, hindi mo na-store yung na-collect mong energy,

**[00:53:36.800] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000594)  
wala kang magagamit, diba?

**[00:53:38.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000595)  
So, hindi siya nagagamit for base load.

**[00:53:41.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000596)  
But now, maybe you can update us on what is the scenario now with the batteries?

**[00:53:49.040] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000597)  
Today, even households can have solar and battery.

**[00:53:52.780] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000598)  
Solar para mag-charge siya ng battery.

**[00:53:55.180] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000599)  
Para pati sa gabi, renewable siya.

**[00:53:57.260] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000600)  
Because battery prices have gone down by 90% in the past 10 years.

**[00:54:02.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000601)  
Okay.

**[00:54:03.080] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000602)  
And Madam Chair, to also add, yung pong ating mga renewables ngayon,

**[00:54:07.360] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000603)  
people are worried na ang mahal naman niyang bagong technology na yan.

**[00:54:11.060] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000604)  
I would like to remind people that when we started solar as fit in 2015,

**[00:54:17.000] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000605)  
the price was ₱9.68

**[00:54:19.560] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000606)  
per kilowatt hour.

**[00:54:20.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000607)  
Okay.

**[00:54:21.040] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000608)  
And today, it's 4.5.

**[00:54:22.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000609)  
Wow.

**[00:54:23.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000610)  
So, from 9.68 to 4.5.

**[00:54:27.240] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000611)  
4.5.

**[00:54:28.200] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000612)  
So, we would like to encourage people not to be afraid of the initial high price of new technologies

**[00:54:33.300] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000613)  
because it will really go down.

**[00:54:35.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000614)  
Okay. Thank you for that, ma'am.

**[00:54:38.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000615)  
So, energy mix.

**[00:54:39.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000616)  
And then, my other question is, what's the fastest growing?

**[00:54:43.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000617)  
So, solar na nga.

**[00:54:44.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000618)  
Solar.

**[00:54:44.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000619)  
So, yung aasahan ko sana dyan is geothermal din, no?

**[00:54:48.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000620)  
Sana.

**[00:54:48.520] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000621)  
Sana. Pero hindi pa.

**[00:54:49.880] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000622)  
Kasi sabi niyo, yung presentation kanina is binabalik pa lang natin ulit eh.

**[00:54:56.080] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000623)  
We are going to borrow money from Asian Development Bank,

**[00:55:00.340] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000624)  
$170 million to de-risk the exploration of wells for geothermal.

**[00:55:06.460] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000625)  
Yun po kasi yung pinaka-risky part eh.

**[00:55:09.220] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000626)  
Kailangan mong mag-drill ng tatlong wells, $6 to $8 million each.

**[00:55:12.840] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000627)  
Okay.

**[00:55:13.120] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000628)  
Lahat sila successful bago mo sabihin na,

**[00:55:15.660] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000629)  
bakit di ako mag-drill dito?

**[00:55:17.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000630)  
Okay.

**[00:55:17.320] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000631)  
And since 2012, when we sold our geothermal plants,

**[00:55:22.420] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000632)  
we have not had greenfield.

**[00:55:24.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000633)  
Since 2012.

**[00:55:26.040] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000634)  
Because the private sector is afraid of the risk.

**[00:55:28.840] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000635)  
So, the geothermal de-risking facility that we are borrowing from ADB

**[00:55:32.500] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000636)  
will basically say, 50% of the risk will be taken by the government.

**[00:55:37.520] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000637)  
So, land bank, our partner, will lend money for exploration, drilling wells.

**[00:55:43.120] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000638)  
Okay.

**[00:55:43.440] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000639)  
If the geothermal developer is successful,

**[00:55:47.320] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000640)  
repay land bank.

**[00:55:49.060] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000641)  
If they're not successful, it's a grant from the Philippine government.

**[00:55:52.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000642)  
So, tumaya ka, but 50% of that is secure ka.

**[00:55:57.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000643)  
So, yung risk mo is just 50%.

**[00:56:01.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000644)  
And it's a loan from ADB by the government?

**[00:56:06.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000645)  
Is that what it is?

**[00:56:07.660] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-000646)  
Yes. Sovereign guaranteed loan.

**[00:56:09.140] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000647)  
Okay. So, that means, basically, yung government yung tumataya.

**[00:56:11.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000648)  
It's just that tinulungan tayo ng ADB to provide the funding.

**[00:56:16.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000649)  
Okay.

**[00:56:17.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000650)  
So, just for your information, Senator Tulfo,

**[00:56:21.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000651)  
there are just two presentations.

**[00:56:22.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000652)  
The first one is done by DOE.

**[00:56:25.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000653)  
The second one is going to be presented to us by Attorney Juan for ERC.

**[00:56:30.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000654)  
And then, after that, I'll open it to questions.

**[00:56:32.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000655)  
So, are you okay with that?

**[00:56:33.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000656)  
Dalawa lang naman tayo, no?

**[00:56:35.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000657)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[00:56:36.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000658)  
Okay. So, Attorney Juan, please proceed. Thank you.

**[00:56:41.220] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000659)  
Thank you, Madam Chair, Honorable Senator Erwin Tulfo,

**[00:56:46.200] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000660)  
and my Secretary Sharon Garin, and my colleagues in the Energy Family.

**[00:56:51.900] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000661)  
I am Attorney Juan, Chairperson and CEO of the ERC.

**[00:56:56.980] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000662)  
And with me are the Commissioners of ERC, Commissioner Floresinda Baldo-Digal,

**[00:57:04.200] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000663)  
Commissioner Amante Liberato, and Commissioner Paris Real.

**[00:57:09.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000664)  
Our mandate in EPIRA is very clear, and that is to promote competition,

**[00:57:15.500] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000665)  
ensure

**[00:57:16.200] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000666)  
consumer choice, protect consumer interests,

**[00:57:19.780] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000667)  
and keep retail rates based on prudent and reasonable cost recovery,

**[00:57:24.640] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000668)  
and penalize abuse of market power.

**[00:57:28.380] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000669)  
Slide three.

**[00:57:29.740] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000670)  
We regulate the full electricity value chain, the regulated sectors,

**[00:57:34.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000671)  
transmission and distribution, and the competitive sector generation and supply.

**[00:57:39.280] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000672)  
So, there can be better appreciation of the following slides as regards the ERC accomplishments

**[00:57:46.200] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000673)  
Let me just describe briefly how we regulate the transmission and distribution sector.

**[00:57:52.540] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000674)  
Basically, we set the rates and performance standards for our transmission utility, the NGCP,

**[00:58:00.600] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000675)  
and our distribution utilities, both the private utilities and the electric cooperatives.

**[00:58:08.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000676)  
For our monitoring and oversight over the competitive sector, namely the generation and supply,

**[00:58:14.500] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000677)  
we basically license it.

**[00:58:16.200] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000678)  
These entities, the generation companies, through the issuance of certificates of compliance, as well as license the retail electricity suppliers through the issuance of the RES licenses to them.

**[00:58:30.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000679)  
At the same time, we issue regulations that will govern the sector short of setting or regulating the prices at which these entities offer to their respective customers.

**[00:58:46.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000680)  
Except that for generation companies that are selling to distribution utilities for their captive market, we review the power supply agreements.

**[00:59:00.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000681)  
that they enter into, as well as for ancillary service providers that are offering ancillary services to the transmission service provider. We likewise review and approve the ancillary service procurement agreements or ASPAs that they enter into. Slide four: the industry we regulate is valued at ₱4.19 trillion, with the DOE projecting around

**[00:59:30.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000682)  
₱20 to ₱31 trillion in investment needs through 2050, and with this it is anticipated that 1.8 million jobs will be created. So this is the scale of the energy sector that our proposed budget of ₱940 million must support. Next slide, the ERC has awoken from its slumber: 5,149 decisions

**[01:00:00.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000683)  
and orders issued in the period which is more than double the 2,411 issued in the same months

**[01:00:09.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000684)  
a year earlier. With the National Lifeline Subsidy Program that ERC developed last year

**[01:00:16.820] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000685)  
in consultation with the DOE and the DSWD, lifeline rate subsidy coverage is up 85.33 percent.

**[01:00:25.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000686)  
It now reaches 620,000 qualified marginalized end users. Net metering registrations are up

**[01:00:33.780] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000687)  
51.77 percent under the streamlined licensing procedures that ERC implemented also last year.

**[01:00:42.920] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000688)  
Worthy to mention also is the completed rate reset of NGCP both for the fourth regulatory period

**[01:00:50.660] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000689)  
and the fifth regulatory period. This happened in 2024.

**[01:00:55.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000690)  
And this happened after more than a decade of delays.

**[01:01:01.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000691)  
And together with the rate reset of NGCP was the approval of 1,602 CAPEX projects worth 290 billion.

**[01:01:13.040] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000692)  
Next slide.

**[01:01:14.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000693)  
The ERC also approved 71.3 billion in refunds as of August 2026.

**[01:01:21.240] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000694)  
More than triple the 21.3 billion.

**[01:01:25.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000695)  
A year earlier, including the 69.95 billion that Meralco was asked to refund

**[01:01:34.480] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000696)  
under the true-up mechanism or the actual weighted average tariff mechanism that ERC devised

**[01:01:44.840] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000697)  
so it can address the period during which there were no rate reset that was conducted for our private distribution.

**[01:01:55.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000698)  
So it is Meralco that was directed but soon because the ERC also directed other private distribution utilities

**[01:02:04.160] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000699)  
to file similar petitions, we will be acting on the other private distribution utilities' AWAT applications.

**[01:02:15.300] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000700)  
The ERC also approved last year 292 power supply agreement applications, up from a mere

**[01:02:25.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000701)  
71

**[01:02:25.920] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000702)  
a year

**[01:02:26.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000703)  
earlier,

**[01:02:27.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000704)  
and 184

**[01:02:28.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000705)  
of those

**[01:02:29.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000706)  
with final

**[01:02:30.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000707)  
authority or decision.

**[01:02:31.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000708)  
Average

**[01:02:32.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000709)  
competitive retail

**[01:02:33.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000710)  
electricity

**[01:02:34.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000711)  
market, or this is the generation

**[01:02:36.460] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000712)  
charge that is usually

**[01:02:37.800] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000713)  
offered by

**[01:02:40.260] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000714)  
retail electricity suppliers

**[01:02:42.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000715)  
to contestable customers,

**[01:02:44.840] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000716)  
the CREM rates are

**[01:02:46.400] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000717)  
₱5.33

**[01:02:48.460] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000718)  
to ₱5.73

**[01:02:50.700] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000719)  
per kilowatt-hour.

**[01:02:52.420] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000720)  
So, all in all, we

**[01:02:54.380] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000721)  
estimate that the CREM

**[01:02:55.900] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000722)  
delivered 19.87

**[01:02:58.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000723)  
billion in consumer

**[01:03:00.000] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000724)  
savings in 2025.

**[01:03:02.680] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000725)  
And related to the

**[01:03:04.380] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000726)  
CREM is the lowering

**[01:03:05.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000727)  
of the threshold. That happened

**[01:03:08.320] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000728)  
also last year.

From the threshold of

**[01:03:12.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000730)

the contestability threshold

**[01:03:16.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000732)  
to 100 kilowatt,

**[01:03:18.120] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000733)  
and then we started,

**[01:03:19.360] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000734)  
and we restarted

**[01:03:21.580] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000735)  
the performance-based

**[01:03:22.840] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000736)

**[01:03:25.440] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000737)  
distribution utilities

**[01:03:26.900] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000738)  
after more than

**[01:03:29.040] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000739)  
a decade of delays

**[01:03:31.100] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000740)  
and made our

mandatory, as I mentioned,

for all private distribution

price for offshore wind and

**[01:03:54.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000752)

**[01:03:57.080] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000753)  
We updated

the AMI rules or the advanced

metering infrastructure rules

**[01:04:01.860] ERC Chairperson and CEO Francis Saturnino C. Juan** (asr-000756)  
for smart grid modernization,

and addressed long-standing

including the public

the PSA and ASPA delayed

**[01:19:08.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001198)

**[01:19:08.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001199)  
Administrator ng National

**[01:19:11.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001200)  
Electrification Administration.

**[01:19:14.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001201)  
Mr. Administrator,

**[01:19:16.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001202)  
Almeda, yung pong

**[01:19:17.680] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001203)

**[01:19:19.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001204)

**[01:19:21.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001205)

**[01:19:23.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001206)

**[01:19:25.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001207)

**[01:19:27.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001208)  
Para saan po ito? Pakipaliwanag nga.

**[01:19:30.640] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001209)  
Your Honors,

**[01:19:31.880] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001210)  
the 7 billion is

**[01:19:33.700] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001211)  
a fund

**[01:19:34.940] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001212)  
in addition to the

**[01:19:37.640] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001213)  
loan equity fund of the

**[01:19:39.820] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001214)  
National Electrification Administration

**[01:19:41.840] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001215)  
to loan to the

**[01:19:44.020] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001216)  
electric cooperatives to cushion

**[01:19:45.920] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001217)  
the effect

**[01:19:47.880] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001218)  
of the elimination of the

**[01:19:49.900] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001219)  
non-technical system losses.

**[01:19:52.400] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001220)  
The elimination

**[01:19:54.040] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001221)  
of the non-technical system losses

**[01:19:55.760] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001222)  
would entail a lot of

**[01:19:57.440] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001223)  
expenditures.

**[01:19:59.940] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001224)  
Case in point,

**[01:20:01.740] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001225)  
the electric cooperatives will have

**[01:20:03.600] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001226)  
to do a lot of metering

**[01:20:05.400] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001227)  
requirements. May metrohan

**[01:20:07.820] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001228)  
po yung lahat ng walang metro.

**[01:20:10.420] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001229)  
Lahat ho ng nagnanakaw

**[01:20:11.960] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001230)  
ay puputulan at lalagyan

**[01:20:13.640] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001231)  
ho ng panibagong metro.

**[01:20:15.800] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001232)  
Yun po ang aming

**[01:20:17.060] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001233)  
na-design yung plano

**[01:20:19.040] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001234)  
that will be involved

**[01:20:21.520] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001235)  
in four stages.

**[01:20:23.660] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001236)  
And the implementation

**[01:20:25.800] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001237)  
will be in phases.

**[01:20:27.320] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001238)  
Kinantify po namin

**[01:20:29.920] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001239)  
ang funding requirement

**[01:20:32.820] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001240)  
para maka-cope up

**[01:20:34.100] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001241)  
ang mga electric cooperatives

**[01:20:35.600] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001242)  
on phase 1, which will be

**[01:20:38.220] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001243)  
the reduction of the

**[01:20:40.020] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001244)  
non-technical system losses to 25%,

**[01:20:42.600] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001245)  
it will entail

**[01:20:43.780] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001246)  
more or less about

**[01:20:45.620] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001247)  
4 billion

**[01:20:46.440] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001248)  
in capital

**[01:20:50.820] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001249)  
expenditure.

**[01:20:53.380] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001250)  
Phase 2,

**[01:20:54.580] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001251)  
pagdating po ng phase 2,

**[01:20:57.160] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001252)  
ang pag-aaral,

**[01:20:57.320] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001253)  
ang projection po namin ay pwede

**[01:20:58.880] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001254)  
nang bawasan ng 50%

**[01:21:00.480] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001255)  
ang non-technical system losses.

**[01:21:02.580] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001256)  
It will again entail about 1 billion

**[01:21:04.900] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001257)  
of capital and expenditure.

**[01:21:08.020] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001258)  
And phase 3,

**[01:21:09.620] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001259)  
pagdumating na po sa phase 3,

**[01:21:11.320] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001260)  
pwede na po natin tanggalin ng 75%

**[01:21:13.960] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001261)  
ng non-technical system losses.

**[01:21:16.420] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001262)  
Another 1 billion.

**[01:21:18.120] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001263)  
And at the last phase,

**[01:21:19.580] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001264)  
where there will be a total elimination

**[01:21:21.740] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001265)  
of the non-technical system losses,

**[01:21:23.640] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001266)  
meaning 100% na po ang tatanggalin natin,

**[01:21:26.160] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001267)  
it will entail

**[01:21:27.200] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001268)  
another 1.5 billion.

**[01:21:28.760] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-001269)  
A total of 7.5 billion, sir.

**[01:21:31.840] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001270)  
Thank you po,

**[01:21:33.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001271)  
Administrator Almeda.

**[01:21:35.140] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001272)  
Now, when I came in,

**[01:22:06.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001288)

**[01:22:10.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001290)

**[01:22:14.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001292)

**[01:22:15.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001293)

**[01:22:20.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001295)

**[01:22:23.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001297)

**[01:22:25.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001298)

**[01:22:27.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001299)  
time lang po eh. Hindi naman daw po ito

basta-basta ibabaon lang sa lupa

**[01:22:31.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001301)

**[01:22:33.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001302)  
Kaya they're looking for ways.

**[01:22:35.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001303)  
Are we doing that now? Are we studying?

**[01:22:37.460] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001304)  
Paano pag 25 years

**[01:22:39.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001305)  
tapos na ito, kailangan ko na palitan,

**[01:22:40.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001306)

**[01:22:51.900] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001311)  
Thank you for that question.

**[01:22:53.360] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001312)  
Meron po tayong Renewable Energy

**[01:22:55.120] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001313)  
Trust Fund wherein we fund

**[01:22:57.080] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001314)

**[01:22:59.260] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001315)

**[01:23:01.460] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001316)

**[01:23:03.280] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001317)

**[01:23:05.600] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001318)

**[01:23:07.240] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001319)

**[01:23:09.180] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001320)

**[01:23:11.100] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001321)  
So by 2030, in place na po yung technologies

**[01:23:13.600] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001322)

**[01:23:15.220] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001323)

**[01:23:17.120] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001324)

**[01:23:19.120] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001325)

**[01:23:21.320] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001326)

**[01:23:23.320] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001327)

Yung ika nga, recycle. Pero

**[01:23:32.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001332)

**[01:23:35.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001333)

**[01:23:36.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001334)  
Hindi naman natin pwedeng ibaon sa lupa

**[01:23:39.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001335)  
o i-tapon.

**[01:23:39.920] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001335-split-2)  
Ang gagawin po

**[01:23:41.160] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001336)

**[01:23:42.900] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001337)

**[01:23:44.540] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001338)

**[01:23:46.880] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001339)

**[01:23:48.760] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001340)

**[01:23:50.840] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001341)

**[01:23:52.700] DOE Undersecretary Rowena Cristina L. Guevara** (asr-001342)

**[02:30:26.120] DOE EUMB Director Patrick T. Aquino** (asr-002974)

**[02:30:32.120] DOE EUMB Director Patrick T. Aquino** (asr-002975)

**[02:30:41.060] DOE EUMB Director Patrick T. Aquino** (asr-002976)

**[02:30:48.940] DOE EUMB Director Patrick T. Aquino** (asr-002977)

**[02:30:53.480] DOE EUMB Director Patrick T. Aquino** (asr-002978)

**[03:28:55.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004387)

**[03:28:57.400] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004388)  
of coal. So

**[03:28:59.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004389)  
and then the remaining balance

**[03:29:01.700] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004390)

**[03:29:03.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004391)  
of others. So we can still

**[03:29:05.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004392)  
improve on that.

**[03:29:11.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004393)  
For energy security, it's not enough

**[03:29:13.680] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004394)  
that we are in survival mode, no?

**[03:29:15.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004395)  
We have to really look into the future.

**[03:29:17.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004396)  
And I need us to see

**[03:29:19.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004397)

**[03:29:21.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004398)  
Nothing fancy,

**[03:29:22.700] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004399)  
because you do have the energy plan.

**[03:29:25.720] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004400)  
10, 20, 30,

**[03:29:27.540] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004401)  
50 years, because if you haven't

**[03:29:29.120] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004402)

**[03:29:31.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004403)

**[03:29:33.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004404)

**[03:29:35.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004405)

**[03:29:37.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004406)

**[03:29:39.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004407)

**[03:29:40.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004408)  
hindi na talaga natin naabutan yun, but siguro

**[03:29:43.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004409)  
that's what our grandchildren,

**[03:29:44.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004410)  
something like that.

**[03:29:47.180] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004411)  
It's being done.

**[03:29:48.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004412)  
So it's not something I'm inventing.

**[03:29:51.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004413)  
That is a practice of countries that

**[03:29:53.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004414)

**[03:29:54.080] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004415)

**[03:29:57.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004416)  
So

**[03:30:00.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004417)  
By investing in long-term security, then we'll have to make tough decisions. That's for sure. And we'll have to allocate limited funds. That's also sure that limited lang yung funds natin. But these are for the programs that will ensure that the average Filipino has, again, access to clean and affordable energy.

**[03:30:19.760] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004418)

**[03:30:30.240] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004419)  
Before you wrap up, I know we have sent a letter with all the possible requests for funding. This includes the system loss for NEA and all that.

**[03:30:43.660] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004420)  
But as a priority, Madam Chair, we would rather have?

**[03:30:49.760] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004421)  
If there's available funding, then that the increase in the ERC funding be done.

**[03:30:56.580] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004422)  
Because sila yung kailangan nila ng faster resolution of all the issues will help also.

**[03:31:04.480] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004423)  
That's the 720 million. We noted that.

**[03:31:07.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004424)  
Yun lang yung…

**[03:31:07.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-004425)  
And kayang-kaya ni SP yan, saka ni Chairman.

**[03:31:11.880] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004426)  
We'll give way to them first. Kung may natirapay, then we can request.

**[03:31:18.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004427)  
Quick question lang.

**[03:31:19.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004428)  
Yung system loss, non-technical yun sa NEA?

**[03:31:22.320] DOE Secretary Sharon S. Garin** (asr-004429)  
Non-technical, yeah.

**[03:31:23.320] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004430)  
Non-technical. And so, the concept, gobyerno mag-absorb yung non-technical?

**[03:31:29.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004431)  
Saka pa-absorb rin ng private sector sa atin, private DU sa atin.

**[03:31:34.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004432)  
Libre nga.

**[03:31:36.040] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004433)  
SP, the non-technical system loss fund request was to add up to the equity loan facility of NEA

**[03:31:45.600] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004434)  
to extend loans to electric cooperatives.

**[03:31:49.280] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004435)  
Hindi po subsidy yun. Yun po ay pandagdag sa equity loan namin para maipautang namin sa mga electric cooperatives

**[03:31:58.300] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004436)  
to upgrade their facilities mainly on their metering requirements.

**[03:32:06.040] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004437)  
So, we found it palatable na hindi po namin ibibigay as a subsidy.

**[03:32:10.800] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004438)  
Pautangin po nila as their CAPEX requirement to upgrade their facility para mawala ho yung non-technical system loss.

**[03:32:18.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004439)  
Non-technical yun.

**[03:32:18.640] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004440)  
Sa non-technical po.

**[03:32:20.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004441)  
Non-technical is pilferage, di ba?

**[03:32:22.660] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004442)  
Well, nung previous hearings po, prinisinta namin, meron po kasi kaming isang case in point na isang electric cooperative

**[03:32:33.080] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004443)  
na napakataas ng system loss, total system loss.

**[03:32:37.460] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004444)  
And we identified po, much of that is non-technical pilferage.

**[03:32:41.460] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004445)  
I am talking about referring to Zamboanga City Electric Cooperative.

**[03:32:44.660] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004446)  
So, when we implemented it,

**[03:32:48.640] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004447)  
a pilot plan from January to July, from 23%, we brought it down to 13%.

**[03:32:56.700] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004448)  
Ang ginawa lang ho namin, hinanap lang ho namin yung walang metro at saka yung mga koneksyon na iligal, minetrohan ho namin.

**[03:33:05.180] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004449)  
So, the same formula po, yun ang gagawin ho namin sa mga ibang electric cooperative.

**[03:33:10.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004450)  
Pilferage, in other words, lalagyan ng metro para magbayad.

**[03:33:15.560] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004451)  
Opo.

**[03:33:15.820] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004452)  
Opo.

**[03:33:16.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004453)  
Okay.

**[03:33:17.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004454)  
Ganun kasimple lang.

**[03:33:18.400] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004455)  
Ganun.

**[03:33:18.640] NEA Administrator Antonio Mariano C. Almeda** (asr-004456)  
Ganun kasimple lang.

**[03:33:19.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004457)  
Okay.

**[03:33:19.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004458)  
Thank you.
